[2026-09-16 18:33] BLOCKER — Docker Desktop 4.81.0 fails to start the engine. `docker info` repeatedly returns "failed to connect to the docker API at npipe:////./pipe/dockerDesktopLinuxEngine ... daemon is running: open //./pipe/dockerDesktopLinuxEngine: The system cannot find the file specified." across three launch attempts (each ~2 min polling). Root cause found in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Docker\log\host\com.docker.backend.exe.log`: "starting services: initializing Inference manager: listening on unix://%USERPROFILE%/AppData/Local/Docker/run/dockerInference: remove %USERPROFILE%/AppData/Local/Docker/run/dockerInference: The file cannot be accessed by the system. (listener: The filename, directory name, or volume label syntax is incorrect.)" — the Docker Model Runner / "Docker AI" inference socket fails to bind because the Windows user profile path contains a space, and the app then auto-clicks "Quit" on its own error dialog and exits. `%APPDATA%\Docker\settings-store.json` has `"EnableDockerAI": true`; flipping it to `false` (app fully quit first) is the likely fix, but editing files outside the project checkout was denied by the local tool-permission classifier ("Irreversible Local Destruction") — this needs the owner to make the change by hand. Separately, the app also spends the first ~30s of every launch downloading/queuing an unrelated 4.91.0 auto-update, which delays every start attempt. This blocks step 3 onward (Sail); it does not block step 2 (git only) (cycle 01 r1)

[2026-09-17 01:45] VERIFICATION + ADVISOR — Plan–repo conflict found: `composer.json` (as generated by the official installer, today) specifies `laravel/framework: ^13.17`; `php artisan --version` confirms **Laravel Framework 13.32.0** — not Laravel 12 as CLAUDE.md/PRD.md/CHECKPOINTS.md/PLAN.md all say. Consulted the advisor (mandatory per HOW-WE-WORK §11, plan–repo conflict) before deciding whether to keep 13 or force a downgrade to 12. What: advisor recommended keeping 13 and disclosing rather than downgrading, gave a discriminating check (`composer.bat show filament/support` → `requires: illuminate/contracts ^11.28|^12.0|^13.0` — Filament 5.8.2 does NOT force 13, it was simply the installer's own current default), reasoned that R9's operative properties (official starter kit · Inertia · Vue 3 · Tailwind 4 · Fortify · Pest) are all satisfied regardless of major version, nothing downstream (partial unique indexes, our own services) depends on the framework major version, and Laravel 12 is now in security-only maintenance so pinning it would ship the MVP on an aging branch. Adopted: kept Laravel 13; did not edit CLAUDE.md/PRD.md/CHECKPOINTS.md/PLAN.md beyond step 0's already-closed, explicitly-authorised edits (none of which named the framework version) — disclosed instead in STATUS.md §6 for the planner to correct in the next revision. Also adopted the advisor's one in-scope code fix: `composer.json` `"php": "^8.3"` → `"^8.4"` (R4: PHP 8.4 everywhere, no fallback — this is a branch code file, not a doc line) (cycle 01 r3)

[2026-09-17 01:50] VERIFICATION — `composer test` (Pint + PHPStan + Pest): first run failed Pint on `bootstrap\providers.php` (line-ending fixer) — fixed with `vendor/bin/pint bootstrap/providers.php`. Second run failed PHPStan: "PHPStan process crashed because it reached configured PHP memory limit: 128M" — fixed by adding `--memory-limit=512M` to the `types:check` composer script (php.ini is outside the checkout; a global edit would also hit the tool-permission classifier, so this is the correct in-repo fix). Third run failed Pint again on `bootstrap\app.php` (line-ending, from `install:broadcasting`'s file write) — fixed the same way. Fourth run failed PHPStan level 7 on `config/horizon.php:72`: `Str::slug()` expects `string`, `env('APP_NAME', 'laravel')` types as `bool|string` — this is Horizon's own stock published config, not app code; per PHPStan's own error instructions ("fix the underlying cause, do not suppress"), cast to `(string) env(...)` rather than add a baseline/ignore comment. Fifth run: **`{"tool":"pint","result":"passed"}` `{"tool":"phpstan","result":"passed","errors":0}` `{"tool":"pest","result":"passed","tests":39,"passed":39,"assertions":136,"duration_ms":20230}`** — composer test fully green (cycle 01 r3)

[2026-09-17 15:02] BLOCKER + DECISION (R16 workaround) — `php artisan migrate --no-interaction` failed: PostgreSQL unreachable on 127.0.0.1:5432 (connection refused). Herd's Postgres/Redis services had stopped (likely the long gap between this run and the prior one — machine sleep or Herd's own idle behavior). `herd.bat services:list` showed both `stopped`; Herd Desktop itself wasn't running either (`herd.bat --version` → "Herd Desktop application is not running"). Relaunched `C:\Program Files\Herd\Herd.exe` (found via the Start Menu `.lnk` target, `HKCU` uninstall registry had no usable `InstallLocation`) — Herd itself became responsive but `herd.bat services:start` for both PostgreSQL and Redis failed. `%APPDATA%\Herd\logs\main.log` showed the real cause: `'postgres.exe' is not recognized as an internal or external command` / same for `redis-server.exe` — Herd's own internal process-spawn PATH doesn't include `~\.config\herd\bin\services\postgresql\18\bin` or `~\.config\herd\bin\services\redis\7.0.0`, even though both binaries exist on disk there and the user's persisted PATH only ever had the top-level `~\.config\herd\bin` (confirmed via `[Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable("Path","User")`) — this must be constructed dynamically by Herd at service-spawn time, and that construction is broken. A second full quit+relaunch of Herd did not fix it, and the app itself crashed once more in between (`net::ERR_FAILED` on license/update/analytics calls in the log — Electron-level network issue, NOT a general connectivity problem: `curl` to github.com and herd.laravel.com from the shell both succeeded). This is a genuine environment bug in Herd Desktop, not something CC can fix (closed-source app, no access to its internal service-spawn code). R16 workaround adopted instead of continuing to wait: found each service's real data directory (`~\.config\herd\config\services\<uuid>\`), removed a stale `postmaster.pid` (referenced a dead PID 3600, confirmed via `tasklist`, left over from Herd's crashed attempt), then started `postgres.exe -D "<datadir>"` and `redis-server.exe "<datadir>\redis.conf"` directly via PowerShell `Start-Process`, bypassing Herd's broken spawner entirely. Both came up clean — Postgres's own log shows a normal WAL-recovery-then-ready sequence from the earlier unclean shutdown, no data loss; Redis `PING` → `+PONG`. Neither service is now managed by Herd's service manager for the rest of this session — if either process dies, it needs re-starting the same manual way, not via `herd.bat services:start`, until the owner looks at why Herd's own spawner is broken. Did not run `services:delete`/`services:create` (would wipe the `elearning`/`elearning_testing` databases; the manual-start path avoided that entirely, no data risk taken) (cycle 01 r4)

[2026-09-17 15:15] DECISION — Own mistake, plainly: R18's text explains the root cause of the whole Herd instability chased at 15:02 — "Launching Herd from CC's shell ties Herd's console to that shell and breaks it when the shell ends." The repeated crashes, the `net::ERR_FAILED` license/analytics errors, and the `postgres.exe`/`redis-server.exe` PATH failures were most likely consequences of CC launching Herd via `Start-Process` from its own Bash-driven PowerShell calls, not an independent Herd bug CC correctly diagnosed. The manual `postgres.exe -D`/`redis-server.exe` workaround at 15:02 remains reasonable given the circumstances at the time (it avoided data loss and unblocked step 4), but CC should not have relaunched Herd itself in the first place, and does not do so again. Step 0b executed: CLAUDE.md `## Local environment` gained the R18 line verbatim (cycle 01 r5)

[2026-09-17 20:48] NOTE — Two real bugs found and fixed while building/testing sub-cycle 1b, disclosed plainly. (1) `TutorProfile::$fillable` was missing `'status'`, so `profileFor()`'s `firstOrCreate()` and the new `CompleteTutorOnboarding` action both silently dropped it via Eloquent's non-strict mass-assignment — every profile's in-memory `status` stayed null/unchanged even though the DB row got the correct default via its own column default. Added `'status'` to `$fillable`; safe because every write path in the codebase sets it from a hardcoded server-side enum value, never from request input. (2) `UserFactory` had no default `'timezone'` (only `'phone'` was added in 1a) — a factory-created tutor's in-memory model kept `timezone` null even though the `users.timezone` column defaults to `'Asia/Dubai'` at the DB layer, so `storeAvailability()`'s server-derived `$user->timezone` hit a NOT NULL violation on `availability_rules`. Not a production bug (the real personal step requires timezone before phone even unlocks later steps) — it only surfaced because tests create tutors past `'personal'` directly. Added the same default `'phone'` already has (cycle 02 r1, sub-cycle 1b)

Also flagged non-blocking items to leave or mention in the PR: `storeAgreement()`'s `firstOrFail()` on a missing seeded page (dev-only, no test impact), the subjects-step tier `<select>` offering all three tiers regardless of curriculum (server rejects; UX-only), and `storeRate`'s plain 409 when no `price_bands` row matches. [2026-09-18 17:20] DECISION — Tutor-status transition table, enforced in the Actions (not only in Filament button visibility) via `TutorStatusTransitionException`: `draft → pending_review` and `changes_requested → pending_review` (tutor, `CompleteTutorOnboarding`); `pending_review → approved` (admin, `ApproveTutor`, and only from `pending_review` — a draft never finished onboarding, a changes_requested profile was not resubmitted and R27 may have cleared its rate); `pending_review | changes_requested → rejected | changes_requested` (admin); `approved → suspended` (admin). Invariant #2's state machine is for lessons; this is the tutor-status analogue. Found by the pre-PR ADVISOR: without it `ApproveTutor` would approve a Draft with a valid permit and `bookable()` would return it, undoing CP1 box 5 through a side door (cycle 02 r3, sub-cycle 1c)

[2026-09-18 17:21] DECISION — `head_scripts` is rendered raw inside `<head>` (`{!! !!}` in `app.blade.php`) by design: DATA_MODEL.md line 169 lists it as "admin-only, rendered raw". Analytics snippets are admin-trusted input; the editor field's helper text says so. Only admins can set it (the panel gate), and admins already hold full control of the site (cycle 02 r3, sub-cycle 1c)

[2026-09-18 17:22] DECISION — "Auto-hide on permit expiry" is `TutorProfile::bookable()` (invariant #5: status approved AND permit strictly in the future), not the scheduled job. `tutors:check-permits` only notifies (30d/7d warnings, expired notice) and never changes status or re-implements bookability; a test asserts an expired-permit tutor is excluded from the scope and keeps status `approved`. The job is idempotent via `audit_logs` rows keyed by (profile, action, permit_expires_at) because DATA_MODEL has no `*_sent_at` columns — a renewed permit starts a fresh cycle, a missed day catches up without double-sending (cycle 02 r3, sub-cycle 1c)

[2026-09-18 17:23] DECISION — Mail sender name/address are read inside each Mailable's `envelope()` at send time (`UsesSettingsSender`), never cached at provider boot — a queued worker boots once and would serve a stale sender after a `site_name` change, breaking CP1 box 8's "next email". `from_name` falls back to `site_name` when unset. Logo/favicon uploads go to the PUBLIC disk: they are branding assets meant to be public (invariant #9 is about tutor documents). New admins are created with `email_verified_at = now()` — an existing admin vouches, and there is no self-registration route to verify through (tested) (cycle 02 r3, sub-cycle 1c)

[2026-09-18 20:40] REVIEW — Fresh-subagent adversarial review of PR #4 (no prior context; given the PR URL, CP1 boxes 4/6/8/9, the tutor-status/money/bookable/private-disk invariants, the forceFill convention, DATA_MODEL line 169, R21's merge rule and the disclosed diff-scope exceptions, with R25's box-7 deferral stated). It cloned the PR head into an isolated directory, ran `composer install`, `npm ci`, `npm run build`, Pint, PHPStan (0 errors), `vue-tsc` (clean) and the suite — 202/205 passing, the 3 failures being `RtlCheckTest`'s WSL-`bash` sandbox artifact (`bash scripts/rtl-check.sh` run directly passed; same artifact as every earlier round). This session's own last local run: **205/205, 686 assertions**. It also wrote and deleted two throwaway probe tests. Sixteen numbered findings:
1. **FAIL (Medium)** — a rejected document can never be replaced. `currentStep()` plucks `document_type_id` from ALL of the profile's documents regardless of status (`TutorOnboardingController.php:411`), so a rejected document counts as "uploaded", and `storeDocument` then aborts 409 because no document type is due (`:136`). Scenario (confirmed by the reviewer's probe): an admin rejects a required document and requests changes; the tutor lands on step `complete`; `POST tutor.onboarding.documents` returns 409; `complete` re-submits to `pending_review` with the document still rejected; approval stays blocked, so the admin can only re-accept the rejected file or reject the tutor. DECISION 15:06's claim that a `changes_requested` tutor can edit "any step exactly as during draft" does not hold for the document step, and no test covers it (`agreementReadyTutor()` deletes all document types). Not an invariant violation, but it defeats CP1's goal ("admin can approve") for the most likely flagged item.
2. PASS — box 4 (`ApproveTutor` sets `approved_by`/`approved_at`, audit row; `TutorApprovalActionsTest` + `TutorProfileResourceTest`).
3. PASS WITH NOTE — box 6 (`DocumentTypeWizardIntegrationTest` drives create/deactivate through Filament; the only hole is finding 1).
4. PASS — box 8 browser title (`app.ts` reads `page.props.name` via Inertia's `(title, page)` callback; verified in the installed Inertia); note: the JS callback itself is untested and the Filament panel's own brand is not wired to `site_name`.
5. PASS WITH NOTE — box 8 email sender read at `envelope()` time on a queued, `SerializesModels` mailable with no in-process settings memo; note: tested by calling `envelope()` directly, not through a real worker, and only the six new mailables use the settings sender — Fortify's verification/reset mails still use `config('mail.from')`.
6. PASS — box 9 (tutor and account owner get 403; role forced server-side; audit row; `canAccessPanel` requires an active account; self-disable and last-admin refused); no Livewire test for the disable table action.
7. PASS WITH NOTE (Low) — a disabled admin keeps some access: the `access-admin-area` gate and `TutorDocumentPolicy`'s admin branch check role only, not status, so a disabled admin can still open an `admin/documents/{document}` URL signed before the disable (≤15 minutes); the last-admin check is not locked/transactional, so two admins disabling each other simultaneously could lock everyone out.
8. PASS (Low note) — tutor-status transitions enforced in the Actions, no back door (`status`/`approved_*` not fillable); the queue's "Submitted" column and default sort use `created_at` (profile creation), not submission time.
9. PASS — invariant #3 (no `round`/float/`toFixed` in the diff; integer fils throughout).
10. PASS (Low note) — invariant #5 and the permit job (scope only, idempotent, renewed permit restarts warnings); one query per approved profile and no `onOneServer`/`withoutOverlapping`.
11. PASS (Low note) — invariant #9 and no bank exposure (admin route signed + admin-gated, tutor route unchanged; nothing in the queue or emails touches bank fields); the `TutorDocumentController` docblock still describes a tutor-only route.
12. PASS (Low notes) — audit content (no secrets); "append-only" is convention only; state change, audit row and event dispatch are not in one transaction.
13. PASS — `head_scripts` (only admins can set it; the only `{!! !!}` in the diff).
14. **FAIL (Low)** — the six events (`app/Events/Tutor/*`) use only `Dispatchable`, not `SerializesModels`, so a queued listener receives a full serialized `TutorProfile` snapshot (including any loaded relation) rather than a fresh re-fetch; adding the trait fixes it.
15. PASS WITH NOTE (Low) — `logo_path`/`favicon_path`/`tagline` are editable but nothing renders them; `SettingsSeeder` overwrites every key, so re-running it would clobber admin edits including `site_name`; the `DatabaseSeederTest` 20→41 change is legitimate.
16. PASS — merge-rule diff scope: only the disclosed exceptions outside the named areas; no protected doc, `ci.yml` or frozen file touched; `create_audit_logs_table` is a new file in this PR, not an edit of a merged migration.
No High findings; one Medium (#1) and one Low in code (#14). Per R21 the Medium halts the programme; per HOW-WE-WORK §6 a Low in code also returns to the owner rather than being fixed in-cycle (cycle 02 r3, sub-cycle 1c)

[2026-09-19 11:05] REVIEW — Second fresh-subagent adversarial review of PR #4 on the R28/R29 head `b5bc351` (fix-loop round 1 of 2; no prior context; briefed to verify the three claimed fixes by tracing and probing, and to hunt for other revisitable-step/downstream-invalidation gaps). It cloned the head into an isolated directory, ran `composer install`, `npm ci`, `npm run build` (exit 0), the full suite — **219 tests, 743 assertions all passing, matching this session's local run** — `bash scripts/rtl-check.sh` directly (pass), and seven throwaway probe tests (deleted; clone clean). Thirteen numbered findings:
1. **FAIL (Medium)** — an admin can reject a required document on an already-approved tutor and nothing reacts. `TutorDocumentsRelationManager.php:48-59` offers Accept/Reject in any profile status; `ReviewTutorDocument.php:14` has no profile-status guard; `hasAllRequiredDocumentsAccepted()` (`TutorProfile.php:150`) is consulted only inside `ApproveTutor`. Probe: approve a tutor, then reject their accepted required document — the tutor stays `approved` and `bookable()` still returns them, while `hasAllRequiredDocumentsAccepted()` is false; the tutor cannot replace it (`currentStep()` returns `submitted` for approved profiles, `TutorOnboardingController.php:394-401`); the only admin recourse is Suspend, which is terminal (no reinstate action). Second scenario, same probe result: adding a required document type while a tutor is approved leaves them bookable. A rejected ID or police-clearance document leaving a tutor bookable is a real consequence — a fourth instance of the recurring class.
2. **FAIL (Medium)** — "request changes" is unusable from the UI for anything except a rejected document. For a `changes_requested` tutor with no rejected document the derived step is `complete`, and `Onboarding.vue:530-537` renders only "You're all set / Submit for review" plus the note — no way to reach the personal, permit, bank, subjects, profile or availability forms. The backend accepts those POSTs (the tests drive them by direct POST only), but the `changes_requested` email tells the tutor to "sign in and update your profile". The reviewer noted this is not one of boxes 4/6/8/9 and left the scope call to the owner. Related: if an admin requests changes without rejecting the document, a pending/accepted document counts as uploaded and cannot be replaced.
3. PASS — claimed fix 1 verified genuine: a rejected document returns the tutor to that type's step; the replacement lands on the right type as `pending`, the other type untouched; a second upload with nothing due returns 409; `complete()` 409s while a required document is rejected; `ApproveTutor` throws until the replacement is accepted. (Probe note: if the rejected document's type is later deactivated, the step is skipped and approval ignores it — consistent with box 6.)
4. PASS — claimed fix 2 verified genuine: a disabled admin is refused by `access-admin-area`, `TutorDocumentPolicy::viewAny` and `::view`, and `canAccessPanel`; probe: 403 on `/admin` and `/admin/tutor-profiles`.
5. PASS — claimed fix 3 verified genuine: `SettingsSeeder` skips any key with an existing row; all six events use `SerializesModels`.
6. PASS WITH NOTE — boxes 4, 6, 8, 9 earned (box 6 driven through `Livewire::test`, not vacuous; box 8's email half asserts `envelope()` directly, not a real send; no browser test of the JS title callback, R20).
7. PASS — invariants and diff scope (status transitions guarded in the Actions, `forceFill`; integer fils; permit job idempotent and not re-implementing bookability; documents private/signed; `head_scripts` the only raw render; no secrets in `audit_logs`; no forbidden file touched; all three migrations new).
8. PASS WITH NOTE (Low) — the disclosed-exceptions list is incomplete: `User.php`, `TutorProfile.php`, `UserStatus.php`, `TutorDocumentPolicy.php`, `TutorOnboardingController.php`, `PriceBandOverlapCheck.php`, `CheckTutorPermits.php` also changed, all serving named deliverables. Acknowledged: these were treated as in-step in the 1a and 1b reviews by the same reasoning; no action.
9. FAIL (Low) as reported — **DISPUTED**: the reviewer says `currentStep()`/`complete()` do not re-derive the agreement version after the page is bumped. That is the specified behaviour: CHECKPOINTS.md CP2 acceptance line 76 — "A tutor who accepted agreement version 1 keeps `agreement_version = 1` after version 2 is published" — and PRD §9 — "publishing a new agreement version does not silently re-bind existing tutors (re-acceptance is a v2 feature; v1 shows admin who accepted which version)". Not a defect; recorded so it is not re-graded on the next pass.
10. FAIL (Low) — `review_note` is never cleared on resubmit or approve (already deferred by the owner in PLAN r4's carried programme-end list).
11. FAIL (Low) — the permit-expiry emails do not match what the system can do: the expiring email tells the tutor to "renew it and update your permit details", but `guardStepNotAhead` returns 409 for approved tutors and Filament has no permit edit, so no renewal path exists (PRD §2.1 line 31 "until a new permit is uploaded and re-approved" is not built); and PRD §8's notification table (line 168) says permit expiring/expired goes to "Tutor + admin" while all six listeners mail only the tutor — CHECKPOINTS line 43 omitted the admin, so this is a genuine spec gap in the six emails.
12. FAIL (Low) — the Vue "Your profile is under review" text shows for every locked status (approved, rejected, suspended alike; the `status` prop is declared but unused), and `SuspendTutor`'s docblock promises the note is "shown to the tutor on next login" while `show()` exposes `reviewNote` only for `changes_requested`.
13. PASS WITH NOTE (Low) — smaller items: document-type `code`/`sort` lack unique/range validation (duplicate code → 500); admin Disable has no confirmation; `ApproveTutor` does not re-check permit expiry or rate at approval; the queue's "Submitted" column is `created_at`; the wizard forces an upload for every active type, not only required ones (pre-existing); replaced files are never deleted from disk; the daily job has no `onOneServer`; suspended/rejected are terminal.
Overall verdict: **do-not-merge** — blocking findings 1 and 2 (Medium). No High. Boxes 4/6/8/9 and the three R28/R29 fixes are genuine; the halt is for two NEW findings in the same recurring class. Per R21, a Medium halts the programme; this would be fix-loop round 2 of 2, which only the owner can authorise (cycle 02 r4, sub-cycle 1c)

R30 LIST — what each revisitable thing invalidates downstream, and how it is re-derived or cleared, one test per dependency: 1. Tutor availability rule/exception edited after slots were shown → nothing cached; next calculation reflects it (test: edit then recompute). 2. `booking_min_lead_hours` / `booking_max_days` changed → read at calculation time (test: change setting, output changes). 3. Tutor suspended/expired-permit → NOT the calculator's job; moved to 2b/CP3 callers with `bookable()` (owed: 2b box-1 test). 4. Recurring slot paused/ended → stops blocking; a `reserved` lesson from it still blocks via `lessons`; a slot with future `starts_on` does not block before it; a passed `ends_on` stops blocking; times interpreted in `slot.timezone` per local date (tests each). 5. Lesson cancelled → slot reopens (terminal statuses excluded from blocking); a lesson at 10:30 blocks both the 10:00 and 11:00 slots. 6. Learner deleted → in 2a: self-learner (is_minor=false) undeletable; parent-created learners soft-deleted; slot/lesson guards owed by CP3/CP4. 7. Learner curriculum/year_group edited → nothing downstream in 2a; owed: 2c match requests and CP3 booking must snapshot or re-validate. Self-learner's nullable `curriculum_id`/`year_group` (R25 deviation: DATA_MODEL is silent, and an adult cannot pick one at registration) means an adult student cannot request a match until they edit the learner — stated for 2b/2c. 8. Tutor changes `users.timezone` → rules keep their own stored zone; `availability_exceptions` have no timezone column, so they are interpreted in the tutor's CURRENT user timezone (drift disclosed, test: rules unchanged, exceptions follow the new zone). Output timezone is an argument, so nothing stored goes stale. 9. User renames themselves → the self-learner `display_name` (= their own name per DATA_MODEL) is synced from the profile-update path (test). Pinned semantics for the test matrix: slot valid when `starts_at >= now + lead_hours` and `starts_at <= now + max_days` (both edges tested); hourly slots step from the rule's `start_time` (09:30–12:30 → 09:30, 10:30, 11:30); Europe/London included for DST; the literal "active recurring slot blocks 10 weeks out" case from box 2 (cycle 02 r5, sub-cycle 2a)

[2026-09-19 14:41] DECISION — `lessons.starts_at`/`ends_at` are `timestamp` (UTC), not `timestampTz`. The first draft used `timestampTz`; the loader test failed because Postgres re-renders `timestamptz` in the session time zone and the `datetime` cast honoured that offset, so a lesson stored at 05:00 UTC read back four hours off and stopped overlapping the slot it should block. `timestamp` plus the UTC application zone is what every other table already uses (`timestamps()`), and DATA_MODEL says `starts_at (utc)`. Caught by the test, not by review; the migration was edited before it was ever pushed or merged (cycle 02 r5, sub-cycle 2a)

(2) Trial price computed in ONE place CP3's `BookLesson` reuses (`TutorProfile::trialPrice()`), server-side, JSON as `Money::format()`, no arithmetic in Vue. (5) Year-group rule stated and its low fidelity disclosed (free text on both sides). (6) Unrated tutors under min-rating: plain reading — excluded; the filter is inert until CP7. (8) Guests use the `default_timezone` setting. (9) The feature middleware is tested with a route defined inside the test, no placeholder CP7 routes. (10) Soft-deleted `?learner=` ids give no prefill. R30 LIST — what each revisitable thing invalidates downstream, and how it is re-derived, one test per dependency: 1. Suspended / expired-permit / permit-expiring-today / draft tutors WITH real availability never appear in search (bookable twin does). 2. Tutor suspended after being listed → search AND profile reflect it on the next request (nothing cached; profile 404s). 3. Tutor edits rate or subjects → search reflects it. 4. A tutor's only slot booked or blocked → drops out of results. 5. `booking_max_days` below 14 shrinks the window (`min(setting, 14)`). 6. `forTutors()` output equals `forTutor()` for every tutor, including two tutors in different timezones in one batch each with an extra exception, and the query count is constant as tutors grow. 7. `?learner=`: null-curriculum learner → no prefill and no error; another parent's learner → ignored; a guest → ignored. 8. Viewer timezone changes the day/time filter and the profile's slot times (Dubai vs London viewer). 9. Feature toggle flip takes effect immediately: 404 off, 200 on; shared `features` prop; reviews block absent when off. 10. Zero-review tutors under a min-rating filter are excluded. 11. Year-group parsing: "Year 8" in ["Year 7","Year 9"] matches, "Year 10" does not, "MYP 3" vs "Year 9" both included (unparseable → permissive), only within the same curriculum. 12. `trial_discount_pct` changed → the profile re-derives per request. 13. `default_timezone` changed → guest results shift. 14. Tutor `users.timezone` differs per tutor within one batch (covered by 6). 15. Soft-deleted learner in `?learner=` → no prefill. 16. (owed, CP7) maintain `rating_avg`/`rating_count`; search reads the stored columns. 17. (owed, CP3) freeze the trial price at booking from `trialPrice()` (cycle 02 r5, sub-cycle 2b)

[2026-09-19 16:06] DECISION — 2b semantics. (a) Year-group rule: extract the first integer from the learner's `year_group` and from `level_min`/`level_max`; compare only within the same curriculum; if either side has no integer the tutor stays in (permissive) — a low-fidelity filter because both sides are free text; a controlled year-group list per curriculum would be a schema change and is out of this cycle (optional Owner action, recommended for the next hand-run cycle). (b) Min-rating: the plain reading — a tutor with no rating is excluded when a minimum is set; PRD §11 is silent; the filter is inert until CP7 maintains `rating_avg`/`rating_count`. (c) Guests: `default_timezone` setting; authenticated: `users.timezone`. (d) Name on the public profile: first token of the account name only (PRD §2.2.3's field list omits the name) — disclosed, optional Owner action (recommended: first name only). (e) `intro_video_url`: an outbound link with `rel="noopener"`, never an iframe. (f) Search window is `min(booking_max_days, 14)`; lead hours still apply. (g) Routes `/tutors` and `/tutors/{tutorProfile}` are public (CP2: "Tutor public profile page"); a non-bookable tutor is a 404. (h) In-memory pagination over the filtered set — fine at v1 scale; revisit if the tutor count reaches the thousands (cycle 02 r5, sub-cycle 2b)

[2026-09-19 19:00] ADVISOR — Sub-cycle 2c design consult (before the first edit). Mandatory triggers named: six new public routes (`/terms`, `/privacy`, `/tutor-agreement`, `/safeguarding`, `/about`, `/contact`), three Filament resources (match requests, pages, content blocks), a shared-prop change in `HandleInertiaRequests` (footer page links), and the touch on 1b's merged agreement flow (`TutorOnboardingController::storeAgreement`, `AgreementStepRequest`, `Onboarding.vue`). Proposed: `match_requests` per DATA_MODEL with a parent create/list flow under `feature:match_requests`, a Filament queue with a "Suggest tutors" action (1–3 tutors), a queued suggestions email; `pages`/`page_versions` (built in 1b) with a Filament editor whose save is Publish and six public routes; `content_blocks` with a Filament editor and a real homepage. Advisor corrections, all adopted: (0) PRD §8 checked — the only match row is "Match suggestions ready → Parent"; there is no admin notification on submit (the queue is the mechanism), so none is built and this is recorded rather than assumed. (1) `bookable()` at THREE points: the Filament picker offers only bookable tutors (filtered to the request's curriculum), `SuggestTutors` rejects any id not bookable at submit (test the smuggled id → refused, status unchanged), and the listener re-filters at send. (2) The suggestions email is built from `TutorPresenter::card()` (first name, headline, rate, trial price, `/tutors/{id}` link), never from the model; a rendered-content leak test (no tutor email/phone/surname/permit); the recipient is the account owner (invariant #7); `goals` are never forwarded to tutors. (3) The agreement echo edits merged 1b code — justified under R30: publishing v2 invalidates an in-flight acceptance rendered against v1, and today the server would record v2, which the tutor never read; the form echoes the version it displayed and a stale OR missing version is rejected (422, the re-render shows the new version). (4) FK cascade trap avoided: `account_user_id` and `learner_id` both `cascadeOnDelete`, `handled_by` `nullOnDelete`; account deletion does not 500. (5) `learner()` uses `withTrashed()` so the queue and the parent page show a soft-deleted learner's name (no name-snapshot column; DATA_MODEL has none). (6) DATA_MODEL has no draft state, so save = Publish is forced: Filament's `MarkdownEditor` (its preview tab is the "preview"), the save action labelled Publish, one transaction with `lockForUpdate()` on the page row so two admins cannot both compute N+1. (7) Markdown via the framework's `Str::markdown($body, ['html_input' => 'strip', 'allow_unsafe_links' => false])`, no new dependency; admins cannot embed raw HTML in public pages (unlike `head_scripts`); tested on the rendered HTML prop. (8) Content-block shapes: `home_hero_title` / `home_hero_text` text, `home_how_it_works` and `home_faq` JSON arrays via a Filament `Repeater`; the renderer tolerates a missing key and malformed JSON. (9) Toggle off freezes open requests (the admin resource is inaccessible until the owner flips it back); a suggestions email already queued when the toggle goes off is still sent (the parent was already promised suggestions and the tutors are re-checked for bookable at send) — pinned by a test; under R20, entry-point absence is proven by the shared `features.match_requests=false` prop and the 404s, not by inspecting Vue. (10) `budget_tier` is a three-value enum (`low|mid|high`) because PRD says only "budget band" — a product question, optional Owner action 4; `preferred_times` free text; `subject_id` required. (11) "Migrated into this structure" is a no-op: 1b built `pages`/`page_versions` on DATA_MODEL's schema and seeded `tutor_agreement` v1; `PageSeeder` becomes insert-missing (an admin's published edit must survive a re-seed, as R29 did for settings) and adds the other five pages; `DatabaseSeederTest` counts grow (pages 1→6, blocks 0→4) — growth, not a removed check. (12) Status transitions are table-driven in Actions: open→suggested (`SuggestTutors`), suggested→suggested (re-suggest replaces the list and updates `suggested_at`/`handled_by`), open|suggested→closed (`CloseMatchRequest`), closed terminal; each refusal tested; audit rows on publish, block edit, suggest and close. Diff-scope files pre-declared: `resources/js/app.ts` (layout for `pages/*`), `HandleInertiaRequests.php` (footer links), `Welcome.vue` (homepage — in scope), `Dashboard.vue` and `tutors/Index.vue` (match entry points), `PublicHeader.vue` + a footer component, the three agreement files, `PageSeeder.php` + `DatabaseSeeder.php`, `routes/web.php`, `app/Providers/Filament/*` if navigation groups change. Build plan: three logical commits (match requests / pages / blocks). [2026-09-20 11:31] DECISION — Step 1 semantics. (a) R32: `TutorProfile::displayName()` splits on `/\s+/u` with `PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY` and takes the first piece; `\s` under `/u` matches U+00A0 and U+3000 in this PCRE build — proven by the passing no-break-space and ideographic-space cases, not assumed; an empty or whitespace-only name gives `'Tutor'`; admin screens (Filament picker, infolist, approval queue) keep the full name on purpose. (b) R34: the cutoff is `permit_expires_at` at UTC midnight, exclusive on `starts_at` — the same day `bookable()` stops counting the permit, so a permit expiring today yields nothing; a null permit is not capped; the permit date is read live, so a renewal reappears on the next calculation. (c) The horizon: 90 days is my number (PRD silent) as `SlotCalculator::MAX_HORIZON_DAYS`, read by the settings editor (`maxValue(90)`) and the loader; a stored `booking_max_days` above 90 is clamped silently by the loader and the editor refuses to save until it is lowered. (d) R35: three text content blocks `match_budget_low|mid|high`, read by one `whereIn` query in `BudgetTierLabels` with the built-in wording as fallback; requests store only the enum value, so relabelling never rewrites history. (e) `UserResource` has no Edit page, so "admin rename via Filament" is not a reachable path today; the observer's `updated` + `wasChanged('name')` on any model save is the guarantee, registered with `#[ObservedBy]`; it is a same-transaction database sync, not a mail, so the Events → queued Listeners convention does not apply; a bulk `User::query()->update()` bypasses model events by design. (f) `Lesson::$fillable` drops `status`; CP3 will `forceFill` it at creation. (g) `forTutors` no longer sets a relation on the caller's models: tutors without a loaded `user` cost one extra query into a local timezone map (5 queries; 4 for eager-loaded input). (h) `CloseMatchRequest` now locks the row inside a transaction like `SuggestTutors` (cycle 03 r1, step 1)

[2026-09-20 13:30] ADVISOR — Cycle 03 step 2 (`cp/2.5b-year-groups`, PR #9, R33) design consult, before the first edit. Mandatory triggers named: public routes (search, and the learner/match/onboarding forms), a data migration on merged tables (`learners`, `tutor_subjects`), and a new Filament resource (year groups). Proposed: a `year_groups` table (curriculum, code, label, sort, level_tier) with 21 seeded rows; `learners.year_group_id` and `tutor_subjects.level_min_id`/`level_max_id` as foreign keys with the old text kept in `*_legacy` columns only where unmapped; `tutor_subjects.level_tier` kept as a stored column derived on save and re-derived when a year group's tier or sort is edited; `match_requests.year_group` kept as a label snapshot; search by `year_group_id`; Filament CRUD. Advisor corrections, all adopted: (1) one source for the 21 rows — a `YearGroupDefaults` class read by both the migration (which inserts for existing curricula) and an insert-missing `YearGroupSeeder`, keyed on `(curriculum_id, code)`; labels pinned: "Year 7"…"Year 13" (GCSE Y7–Y11, A-Level Y12–Y13), "MYP 1"…"MYP 5", "DP 1"/"DP 2", and CBSE "Grade 6"…"Grade 12" (a default the admin can relabel through the CRUD); tiers per row satisfy `CurriculumCode::tiers()` (GCSE Y7–9 lower_secondary, Y10–11 exam_1; A-Level exam_2; MYP 1–3 lower_secondary, 4–5 exam_1; DP exam_2; CBSE 6–8/9–10/11–12). (2) The `*_legacy` columns are an R25 deviation (R33 says the columns "become foreign keys"; DATA_MODEL has no legacy text) — justified because the report and any correction need the original text; disclosed and listed for the v1.3 note; `docs/DATA_MODEL.md` is NOT edited in step 2 (R37 puts it in step 5), and R39's protected list is CLAUDE.md, HOW-WE-WORK.md, PRD.md and `ci.yml` — `docs/reports/` is expected. (3) The mapping is trimmed, case-insensitive exact-label within the row's curriculum — a relaxation of R33's "exact label", disclosed; the matrix is tested directly on `LegacyYearGroupMapper` over post-structural rows (ids null, legacy text set), plus one rollback → seed legacy text → migrate round trip because the plan asks for "the migration on a seeded copy" (`down()` exists for that test; migrations stay forward-only once merged). (4) The report carries no names: `year-groups:report` prints table, row id, curriculum code and legacy text only, never a `display_name` or tutor name; the committed `docs/reports/year-groups-migration.md` records the local run (expected 0 unmatched). (5) "Left null for admin correction" has no admin path — there is no admin learner editor, and tutor subject rows are edited only in onboarding while approved tutors are locked — so in practice the report identifies rows and the owner of the row fixes it (a parent through the learner form, a draft/changes_requested tutor through onboarding); approved tutors with unmapped rows stay permissive in search until R36(b)'s re-vetting path lands in step 3; no editor is built. (6) Filament delete: a raw FK restrict would be a 500, so Delete is hidden when any learner or tutor subject references the year group, refused with a notification if called anyway, the FK as backstop; `level_tier` must be one of the curriculum's tiers; curriculum immutable after creation; `(curriculum_id, code)` and `(curriculum_id, label)` unique with friendly messages. (7) Tier or sort edit re-derives `tutor_subjects.level_tier` (rule: highest tier by `sort` among the curriculum's year groups in `[min.sort, max.sort]`; min.sort ≤ max.sort; same curriculum as the row; a client-sent `level_tier` is dropped from the request rules), writing one audit row with the count of rows changed — consequence disclosed: this can push an APPROVED tutor's rate out of band and nothing re-checks until R36(f) at approval (step 3) and CP3 at booking. (8) Search: the year-group filter needs a curriculum (the dropdown is dependent; with "Any" it is disabled and ignored); SQL `whereHas('tutorSubjects', curriculum match AND (min_id null OR min.sort ≤ chosen.sort) AND (max_id null OR max.sort ≥ chosen.sort))`; `?learner=` prefill uses the learner's `year_group_id` only when the curriculum filter is empty or equals the learner's curriculum; `TutorPresenter::subjects()` shows labels with a legacy-text fallback. (9) The dependent selects touch four forms — `learners/Form.vue`, `match-requests/Create.vue`, `tutors/Index.vue`, `tutor/Onboarding.vue` — verified under R20 by build/type-check and by the server rules that reject cross-curriculum ids. (10) L4: strip only LEADING `\p{Cf}` characters (U+200B, U+200C, U+200D, U+FEFF, U+200E/F) before splitting, never split on an internal ZWJ/ZWNJ (Persian and Indic names rely on them). (11) L2: a rule at 04:00 `Asia/Dubai` gives a slot at exactly 00:00 UTC; a permit expiring that day refuses it and the 23:00 UTC slot the day before is offered. (12) Test integrity: existing tests change shape, not just names — every onboarding subjects POST (including `agreementReadyTutor()` in `tests/Pest.php` and the R26/R27 band tests), `TutorSubjectFactory`, `LearnerFactory`, `MatchRequestFactory`, `TutorSearchTest`'s year-group test (the free-text parsing semantics are removed by R33, so that test is replaced, not weakened), `DatabaseSeederTest` (+21 year groups); assertions are unchanged except where R33 changes the behaviour under test. R30 LIST — one test per dependency: 1 a learner's curriculum change requires a year group of the new curriculum, a year group of another curriculum is rejected. 2 a referenced year group cannot be deleted (hidden and refused), an unreferenced one deletes. 3 a label/code edit shows everywhere by join, except the match-request snapshot which is unchanged by design. 4 a tier or sort edit re-derives `level_tier`, with one audit row and the changed-row count. 5 onboarding rejects min > max, another curriculum's ids and a client-sent tier; a mixed-tier range takes the highest tier. 6 search by `year_group_id`: in-range included, out-of-range excluded, null legacy ids permissive, curriculum required. 7 a match request needs a year group of its curriculum, stores the label, and a later rename does not change it. 8 the mapper matrix (trimmed, case-insensitive exact label; unmatched left null with legacy text; null-curriculum learners unmatched; idempotent) and the rollback/re-run round trip. 9 the seeder is insert-missing, 21 rows, after `CurriculumSeeder`. 10 Filament CRUD is admin-only and audited, curriculum immutable, tier validated. 11 the presenter shows labels with a legacy fallback. 12 a draft or changes_requested tutor with unmapped rows lands back on the subjects step (approved tutors are locked). 13 a parent with a null-year-group minor must choose one on the next edit. 14 L2 boundary and L4 zero-width names. 15 prefill with a curriculum mismatch is ignored. 16 an approved tutor's rate may fall out of band after a tier edit — owed to R36(f)/CP3, made explicit. 17 the report carries no names. 18 `YearGroupSeeder` insert-missing and the seed-count growth. Diff-scope files pre-declared: the migration, `YearGroupDefaults`, `LegacyYearGroupMapper`, `YearGroupSeeder`, `DatabaseSeeder`; `YearGroup` model and factory; `Learner`, `LearnerController`, both learner requests, `learners/Form.vue`, `learners/Index.vue`; `TutorSubject`, `TutorSubjectFactory`, `SubjectsStepRequest`, `TutorOnboardingController` (`storeSubjects`, `currentStep`, `show` props), `Onboarding.vue`; `TutorSearch`, `TutorSearchCriteria`, `TutorSearchRequest`, `TutorSearchController`, `TutorPresenter`, `tutors/Index.vue`, `tutors/Show.vue`; `StoreMatchRequestRequest`, `MatchRequestController`, `match-requests/Create.vue`; `app/Filament/Resources/YearGroups/*`; `app/Console/Commands/YearGroupsReport.php`; `docs/reports/year-groups-migration.md`; `TutorProfile.php`, `TutorDisplayNameTest` (L4), `PermitCapTest` (L2), `tests/Pest.php`. Mechanics: three commits (schema/migration/seeder/report · onboarding/search/learners/match · Filament + L2/L4); after a clean review R39 lets Claude Code merge PR #9 itself (no Medium/High, CI green, diff within the step's areas plus tests/migrations/seeders/factories/routes/resources, none of CLAUDE.md, HOW-WE-WORK.md, PRD.md, `ci.yml` touched), then straight into step 3; a Medium+ keeps the PR open and halts "awaiting owner" (cycle 03 r2, step 2)

[2026-09-20 19:50] DECISION — Step 3 design pinned by the consult (cycle 03 r2, step 3): (1) Fortify mails: two subclasses `App\Notifications\Auth\VerifyEmailNotification` and `ResetPasswordNotification` extending Laravel's, `ShouldQueue`, using `UsesSettingsSender` (from + reply-to read at send time, fallback to config); `User` overrides `sendEmailVerificationNotification` and `sendPasswordResetNotification`. Because `NotificationFake` keys on the exact class, the assertions in `tests/Feature/Auth/PasswordResetTest.php` (3) and `VerificationNotificationTest.php` (1) that name the stock class are RETARGETED to the subclass (same strength; each also asserts it is an instance of the stock class) — pre-disclosed test reshape. (2) `TutorProfile::permitIsValid()` is the one PHP predicate (`permit_expires_at` strictly after today, the same rule as `bookable()`), with a boundary test that it agrees with the scope on yesterday/today/tomorrow. (3) `TutorRateBands` (extracted from `TutorOnboardingController::rateBandFor/currentBandsFor`, integer fils) — no band, a conflicting band, or a rate outside [min, max] all REFUSE approval/reinstate-to-approved with a named reason; `TutorOnboardingBankSubjectsRateTest` must not change. (4) The Filament "Request changes" header action becomes available on `approved` tutors (the new edge exists; simplest; disclosed); `approved_at`/`approved_by` are kept when a tutor leaves `approved` (history; overwritten on re-approval); reinstate → approved sends NO email (suspend sends none), reinstate → changes_requested sends the changes-requested email. (5) A newly required/activated document type fans out to APPROVED tutors only. NOT included, disclosed as owed: `pending_review` tutors missing the new document can be neither approved nor upload (the admin can move them with "Request changes", which is allowed) — owed to a later plan; a price-band edit and turning `required` off re-derive nothing (a tutor moved by an earlier required-toggle is not moved back). (6) `DocumentType::PERMIT_CODE = 'permit'`; (g) no-ops when there is no permit document type or scan; document-type `code` becomes immutable on edit (behaviour change beyond "validated", disclosed) so (g) cannot be silently broken. (7) Transaction/lock/after-commit is implemented on ONE action first (ApproveTutor), `TutorEventsQueueSafetyTest` and `TutorApprovalActionsTest` run, and the after-commit dispatch under RefreshDatabase proved with a test, before the other actions get it; `TutorStatusTransitionException` is still what is thrown. (8) `submitted_at`: nullable timestamp, backfilled from `created_at` for non-draft rows, set at every submit; the queue "Submitted" column sorts on it. (9) Last-active-admin check: `DB::transaction` + `lockForUpdate` on the active admin rows; a genuine concurrent race cannot be proven in Pest, so the test asserts the lock/transaction structure and the sequential refusal, and I say so. (10) Commits: (1) transitions + reinstate + re-vetting + `submitted_at`/`review_note`; (2) (d)(g)(e)(f) + `TutorRateBands`; (3) the six Lows. Pre-declared file list (the reviewer reconciles against it): `app/Services/Tutors/{TutorStatusTransitions,TutorRateBands}.php`, `app/Actions/Tutor/{ApproveTutor,RejectTutor,RequestTutorChanges,SuspendTutor,ReinstateTutor,CompleteTutorOnboarding,ReviewTutorDocument,RequireDocumentTypeFromApprovedTutors}.php`, `app/Exceptions/TutorStatusTransitionException.php`, `app/Models/{TutorProfile,DocumentType,User}.php`, `app/Notifications/Auth/*`, `app/Http/Controllers/Tutor/TutorOnboardingController.php`, `app/Actions/Admin/DisableAdminUser.php`, `app/Console/Commands/CheckTutorPermits.php` if touched, `routes/console.php`, `app/Filament/Resources/{TutorProfiles,DocumentTypes,PriceBands,Users}/**`, `database/migrations/2026_09_20_110000_add_submitted_at_to_tutor_profiles.php`, factories, and new/edited tests under `tests/Feature/{Tutor,Filament,Auth,Scheduling}` (cycle 03 r2, step 3)

Done after the consult: (1) `ReviewTutorDocument::__invoke` now runs in one transaction on the profile row locked for update, decides on the locked status, keeps `deleteReplacedFiles` on `DB::afterCommit`, and dispatches `TutorChangesRequested` after the commit; `RequestTutorChanges` gained a public `apply()` (the transition without the event) that it calls inside that transaction; three new tests (stale page: pending_review in memory, approved behind it → ends `changes_requested`; the mirror, suspended behind it → refused and nothing written; a forced failure of the tutor move rolls the document decision back and dispatches nothing); (2) both vacuous assertions removed/replaced (the subject is asserted as the real `Verify your email address`); the "vanishes" test is an HTTP test on the PUBLIC PROFILE page (`tutors.show` 200 → 404), not on `/tutors` search — the search-side proof of invariant #5 is step 2's; disclosures are in STATUS §6 and the PR body. [2026-09-21 07:50] ADVISOR — Ledger design consult (advisor #2 of 3b, its own entry because it decides ledger entries, per the plan). Put to the advisor: DATA_MODEL v1.3's ledger table does not sum to zero per lesson as R52 requires — HOLD is a bare `escrow +price`, so a lesson sums to `+price` after HOLD, after HOLD+RELEASE and after HOLD+REFUND. Proposed: a fifth account `gateway` as the external counter-leg (HOLD = `gateway −price`, `escrow +price`), RELEASE split into two zero-sum pairs (escrow −tutor_amount / tutor +tutor_amount; escrow −commission_amount / platform +commission_amount), REFUND `escrow −price`, `refund +price`, SETTLE and PAYOUT declared and unused, preconditions inside a locked transaction. Came back: the arithmetic is right and no reading of the literal table sums to zero, but the gap is narrower than I framed it — only HOLD lacks a leg (RELEASE `−price + t + c` = 0 because `t + c = price`; REFUND = 0; SETTLE = 0; PAYOUT carries `payout_id`, not `lesson_id`, so it is outside the per-lesson sum); so the `gateway` leg is a one-line correction to the table, and the RELEASE split is a SECOND, independent deviation, justified not by the invariant but by the `type` column (a single `escrow −price` has no honest type from hold|release_tutor|release_commission|refund|goodwill|payout) — disclose it separately; keep the name `gateway` and expect a `bank` account at CP5 (do not use a generic `external`); the preconditions need something to lock and `ledger_entries` has no row to lock, so lock the LESSON row at the top of the transaction and do the SUM inside it, the same shape as `TutorStatusTransitions::transition()`; verify the tutor balance buckets still read only `tutor`-account and `payout`-type entries (they do); after HOLD+REFUND the two external legs (`gateway −price`, `refund +price`) do not net in one account but the per-lesson sum is 0 — explain that in v1.4; two new enums `LedgerAccount` and `LedgerEntryType` built into the migration with `array_column(…::cases(), 'value')`; the `gateway` account belongs in ADR-008 as well as v1.4. Adopted in full.

[2026-09-21 07:55] DECISION — 3b design pinned. (1) Scope: 3b creates only `lessons` (extended), `tutor_strikes`, `ledger_entries`. `lessons.payment_method_id`, `ledger_entries.payout_id` and `ledger_entries.dispute_id` are plain nullable `unsignedBigInteger` columns WITHOUT foreign keys (their tables — `payment_methods`, `payouts`, `disputes` — arrive in CP5/CP8; the constraints are added by later forward-only migrations); `payments` is 3c's table. (2) `lessons` is extended with `Schema::table`, never replaced; the migration refuses (with a plain exception) to run if the table already holds rows, because the new NOT NULL columns have no honest default — it is empty on every server (rehearsal `db:show`: 0 rows). Both partial unique indexes: the slot index exists (CP2 `lessons_tutor_slot_unique`, predicate `freeingSlotValues()`) and is NOT touched — a test reads the live predicate from `pg_get_indexdef` and compares it with the enum; the new trial index is `(learner_id, tutor_profile_id) WHERE type = 'trial' AND status NOT IN (cancelled states)` using the same set, so a refunded or cancelled trial does not consume the trial (tested). (3) Statuses: `LessonStateMachine` (`app/Services/Lessons/`) declares all 17 states and every edge in the DATA_MODEL diagram — pending_payment → confirmed|expired; reserved → confirmed|cancelled_payment_failed|cancelled_by_parent|cancelled_by_tutor; confirmed → cancelled_by_parent|cancelled_by_tutor|in_progress|no_show_both|provider_failure; in_progress → completed|no_show_student|no_show_tutor; completed → completed_reported|disputed; completed_reported → disputed; disputed → settled; cancelled_by_parent → refunded|completed_reported; and the documented auto-advances no_show_student → completed_reported, no_show_tutor|no_show_both → refunded — and no edge the diagram does not draw (in particular no `cancelled_by_tutor → refunded`: the diagram ends a tutor cancel at `cancelled_by_tutor` with a REFUND ledger effect; 3d confirms that reading). API: `open(attributes, initial)` (initial only pending_payment or reserved), `transition(lesson, to, ?work)` — one `DB::transaction`, the lesson row locked, the caller's stale copy synced, the edge asserted on the LOCKED status, `work` run inside the same transaction, the event dispatched after commit; `LessonTransitionException` on a forbidden edge. ONE event, `App\Events\Lessons\LessonStatusChanged(lesson, ?from, to)` (from null on creation) — R51's "each firing its event" is met by that event carrying the edge; no per-status classes without listeners. Writes to `lessons.status` are guarded twice: a `saving` hook on `Lesson` throws when `status` is dirty outside the machine's scope, and a static test scans `app/` for status writes on `Lesson` outside `LessonStateMachine`; factory-side and test-side writes (`LessonFactory`, `Lesson::query()->update([...])` in three existing tests) are exempt because tests must be able to build any state — the factory opens the same scope, and the scan is scoped to `app/` only. (4) Ledger (its own consult above): accounts `gateway|escrow|tutor|platform|refund`, types `hold|release_tutor|release_commission|refund|goodwill|payout`; HOLD = gateway −price, escrow +price; RELEASE = escrow −tutor_amount, tutor +tutor_amount, escrow −commission_amount, platform +commission_amount; REFUND = escrow −price, refund +price; `settle`/`payout` declared, throwing `LogicException` until CP5; every op is a `DB::transaction` that LOCKS THE LESSON ROW first (there is no ledger row to lock), checks its precondition (HOLD: no hold yet; RELEASE/REFUND: the lesson's escrow balance equals the price), checks `commission_amount + tutor_amount = price`, writes the legs, then re-sums the lesson and throws (rolling back) unless it is zero. `ledger_entries` is append-only: `UPDATED_AT = null`; the model throws on `updating` and `deleting`, a `creating` hook throws outside `LedgerService`, and the migration adds a Postgres trigger that rejects UPDATE and DELETE — two tests prove both throw. `ledger:verify` lists every lesson whose entries do not sum to zero and exits 1 if any; it is added to the composer `test` script AFTER Pest (so the CI database is already migrated) and passes vacuously on an empty database — the proof of the invariant is the zero-sum assertion in every money test. (5) `Money` is not extended in 3b (no operation is missing; the commission/tutor split arrives in 3c as one subtraction). (6) Files (the reviewer reconciles the diff against this list; the frozen files LedgerService, LessonStateMachine and the ledger migration are CREATED here — R52; they freeze after the checkpoint closes): migrations `2026_09_21_1*_extend_lessons_table.php`, `_create_tutor_strikes_table.php`, `_create_ledger_entries_table.php`; enums `LessonType`, `StrikeType`, `LedgerAccount`, `LedgerEntryType`; models `Lesson` (extended), `TutorStrike`, `LedgerEntry`; `app/Services/Lessons/LessonStateMachine.php`, `app/Services/Ledger/LedgerService.php`; `app/Exceptions/{LessonTransitionException,LedgerException}.php`; `app/Events/Lessons/LessonStatusChanged.php`; `app/Console/Commands/VerifyLedger.php`; factories `LessonFactory` (extended), `TutorStrikeFactory`; `composer.json` (`test` script); tests under `tests/Feature/Lessons`, `tests/Feature/Ledger`, `tests/Unit` as needed. Test shifts pre-declared: `LessonFactory` grows required columns (learner, curriculum, subject, price, policy), so the scheduling and search tests that use it keep working only if the factory fills them — verified by the existing suite.

[2026-09-23 19:20] DECISION — Commission-split formula for `Money`, per the advisor, correcting the design floated in the first consult: `Money::percentage()` (half-up rounding) is NOT used for the commission split, because it can give the tutor the remainder fil instead of the platform, violating R53. New method computes `tutor = intdiv(price->toFils() x (100 - commission_pct), 100)`, `commission = price->subtract(tutor)`, so `commission + tutor = price` exactly and any remainder fil lands on the commission/platform side by construction (subtraction, not a second rounding). Tested at 12345 fils / 25% (tutor 9258, commission 3087) and at 101 fils / 25% — prices where the naive half-up formula would have given a different, wrong split. `trialPrice()` is untouched; it remains the sole trial-price computation per R53.

[2026-09-23 19:20] DECISION — Tutor-overlap enforcement for the booking migration, per the advisor and the VERIFICATION above: 3b's merged `lessons_tutor_slot_unique` (partial unique index on `(tutor_profile_id, starts_at)`, from the CP2 stub) does not prevent two bookable-but-overlapping lessons for one tutor when their availability rules start on different offsets — proven by `SlotCalculator.php:35`'s own "a lesson at 10:30 removes both 10:00 and 11:00" blocking rule, which only works pre-booking because it checks interval overlap in memory; nothing enforces the same rule in the database once two different `starts_at` values are both committed. Fix: a new forward-only migration (not an edit to the merged 3b/CP2 migrations) adds `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist` (confirmed available locally, see VERIFICATION above) and `ALTER TABLE lessons ADD CONSTRAINT lessons_tutor_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (tutor_profile_id WITH =, tstzrange(starts_at, ends_at) WITH &&) WHERE (status NOT IN (freeing set))`, using `LessonStatus::freeingSlotValues()` for the predicate so the app check, the trial index and this constraint all agree on which statuses free a slot. The existing `lessons_tutor_slot_unique` index is left in place (harmless, still useful for the exact-instant fast path) rather than dropped, to keep this migration additive and low-risk. `BookLesson`'s transactional check plus this constraint together satisfy CP3 box 3 and PLAN step 3's "overlap ... held by the DB indexes AND a transactional check", corrected to mean this new exclusion constraint rather than the pre-existing unique index alone.

[2026-09-23 19:45] ADVISOR — Third consult this session, money-touching trigger (freezing/HOLD sequencing for `BookLesson`, plus a request for a final sanity check on the full file plan before writing code). Model: not reported by the tool; configured advisor per PROJECT_BRIEF is Fable 5.1 — configured, not measured. Confirmed answering. Advice, in full, five points: (1) withdrew the earlier `tstzrange` overlap-constraint design — `lessons.starts_at`/`ends_at` are `timestamp(0) without time zone` (Postgres column type), so `tstzrange()` needs an implicit timestamp→timestamptz cast that is STABLE (depends on session TimeZone), which Postgres refuses inside a GiST index expression ("functions in index expression must be marked IMMUTABLE"); use `tsrange(starts_at, ends_at, '[)')` instead, half-open bounds so 09:00-10:00 and 10:00-11:00 coexist; also an exclusion violation raises SQLSTATE 23P01, not 23505 — exception mapping must handle both codes by constraint name. (2) the logged txn2 sequence breaks R52 ("payments rows are written for every capture"): writing the `payments` row inside `LessonStateMachine::transition()`'s `$work` closure means a refused transition (e.g. the sweep expired the lesson between capture and confirm) rolls the insert back with it, leaving captured money with no record; fix: write the `payments` row (status `captured`) in its own commit immediately after capture, then run the transition + HOLD as a separate step; add a test that expires the lesson between capture and confirm and asserts a captured `payments` row exists, the lesson is `expired`, and there is no HOLD; log the captured-but-expired refund gap as a CP5 carried item (needs the real gateway); DATA_MODEL's `lessons ||--o| payments` justifies a unique index on `payments.lesson_id`, which also gives idempotency. (3) concurrency test mechanics: `tests/Pest.php` binds `RefreshDatabase` to all of `tests/Feature`, so adding `DatabaseTruncation` in a Feature-tree file stacks both traits and setup rows stay uncommitted/invisible to child processes; put the test in its own directory with its own `pest()->...->in()` binding, add that directory to `phpunit.xml`'s testsuite discovery (disclosed under R70's test-tooling scope), and note the gateway DECISION leaves no app binding, so each child process's entry script must bind `FakePaymentGateway` itself to boot `BookLesson`; test both identical `starts_at` and an offset overlap (09:30 vs 10:00) — only the offset case proves the new constraint. (4) flagged DATA_MODEL's "Overlap protection" section (read but not yet decided) as still open: does learner-side overlap need its own exclusion constraint, or does the existing non-unique `(learner_id, starts_at)` index stand — asked in consult 1, still unanswered at time of this consult. (5) STATUS should be rewritten before the build (cheap, since the branch had no commits yet): §4 gets the three DECISIONs, §6 gets the gateway-deferral line, the overlap-index correction to PLAN step 3's wording, a risk note that `migrate --force` on rehearsal runs `CREATE EXTENSION` for btree_gist (CC cannot verify the forge role's rights, raw SQL being on the never-list), and the §1 commit-count fix. Smaller non-blocking points: Box 5 test must vary all five settings plus `hourly_rate` plus the price band between booking and capture, then assert all seven frozen columns and the HOLD amount are unchanged; onboarding preview — grep `resources/js` for `trialDiscountPct` (found: `resources/js/pages/tutor/Onboarding.vue`), if it updates live from a candidate rate use a transient `TutorProfile` instance's `trialPrice()` rather than changing `trialPrice()`'s signature; band check reuses `TutorRateBands::problemWithRate()`, no new check; `LessonFactory`'s docblock phrase "tutor amount the remainder" describes the rejected formula and needs updating to match the corrected one; learner-ownership check in `BookLesson` follows `CreateMatchRequest`'s pattern (not trashed, `account_user_id` matches the booking user, invariant 7). Sequencing given: item 1 blocks the overlap migration; item 2 blocks `BookLesson`'s capture code; item 4 blocks the migration; item 5 before any code commit; the `Money` split and the gateway interface can start now.

(2) missing pieces not yet listed: no `App\Models\Payment` model exists yet — needs one plus a `PaymentFactory` (CLAUDE.md requires a factory for every model) with casts `amount`→`Money`, `status`→`PaymentStatus`, `raw_response`→array; `lessons.curriculum_id`/`subject_id` are NOT NULL and nothing supplies them yet — take both as `BookLesson` inputs, validate a `tutor_subjects` row exists for (tutor, curriculum, subject). (3) `BookLesson` drafting gotchas: UTC storage — Laravel's datetime cast formats a Carbon in its own timezone without converting, so a Dubai-zoned or slot-output-timezone Carbon must be `->utc()`'d explicitly before `LessonStateMachine::open()`, matched against `SlotCalculator::forTutor()`'s slots by `getTimestamp()` not by string/zone comparison, with a test booking from an `Asia/Dubai` input and reading the raw `starts_at` via `DB::table`; re-fetch the tutor fresh via `TutorProfile::query()->bookable()->whereKey($id)->first()` (invariant 5's named scope) rather than trusting the caller's possibly-stale in-memory model, and pass that fresh instance into `SlotCalculator`; the trial/regular query must NOT filter on `type` — `learner_id` + `tutor_profile_id` + `whereNotIn(status, freeingSlotValues())` only, else a specific sequence (trial confirmed → regular booked → trial cancelled by tutor) gets a second discount that the `type='trial'`-scoped DB index would not catch; translate constraint violations (23505 or 23P01, matched by SQLSTATE and constraint name) outside the `DB::transaction()` call — 23P01 is a plain `QueryException`, not `UniqueConstraintViolationException`; guard the capture-failure → `expired` transition against a sweep that already expired the lesson (catch `LessonTransitionException`, since `expired` has no outgoing edges and a second transition throws), same for the capture-success → `confirmed` path racing the sweep. (4) row-lock-vs-constraint choice for the box-3 concurrency test: if `BookLesson` locks the tutor row, parallel calls serialize and the DB constraint itself never fires under test, proving the lock instead of "the DB constraint" box 3 asks for — either choice is valid design, but the constraint itself needs its own deterministic Pest test (offset → 23P01, identical start → 23505, adjacent → OK, freeing statuses → OK, all via savepoints) committed as an automated test, not left as the manual tinker proof above. (5) test technique: bind test-only `PaymentGateway` doubles to deterministically reach every branch (box 5's freeze proof varying all five settings + `hourly_rate` + the band between booking and capture; the capture-then-expire race asserting a `captured` payments row, an `expired` lesson, and no HOLD entries; a throwing double for the plain capture-failure path) rather than relying on real concurrency for all of them. (6) plan/doc items: R57 requires a DATA_MODEL v1.4 deviation note for `lessons_tutor_no_overlap` since it goes beyond DATA_MODEL:130's stated overlap section — blocks PR #13; box 5 literally says "does not change the *outcome*", and outcome (cancellation) is 3d scope — 3c can only prove the freeze, so the box should not be ticked fully closed yet, note the split in STATUS §6 (non-blocking); PLAN step 3 names no controller/route/page — keep 3c at action + command + tests, note the missing booking entry point in STATUS §6 rather than building one; the expiry sweep should `transition()` per row catching `LessonTransitionException`, run `everyMinute()->withoutOverlapping()->onOneServer()`, have a run-twice test, and cite "15 minutes" as a class constant rather than inventing a new settings key; non-blocking — decide explicitly whether a failed (non-exception, gateway-declined) capture writes a `payments` row with status `failed` and a `failure_reason`, and log the decision either way.

[2026-09-23 20:10] DECISION (Claude Code, per advisor item 6) — A gateway-declined capture (caught `PaymentCaptureException`, e.g. `card_declined`) writes its own `payments` row before the lesson is transitioned to `expired`: `status: failed`, `failure_reason` set from the exception message, `gateway_ref: null`. Reason: box 5/PLAN step 3c wants a readable audit trail for every capture attempt, not just successful ones, and `ExpireUnpaidLessons`'s "unpaid" predicate (`whereDoesntHave('payment', captured)`) already has to look at the `payments` table anyway — a failed row costs nothing extra and gives support/disputes a record of *why* a lesson expired instead of only *that* it did. Implemented in `app/Actions/Lessons/BookLesson.php`'s `catch (PaymentCaptureException $e)` block (writes the row, then attempts the `expired` transition, then rethrows). Covered by `BookLessonTest.php`'s capture-declined test (asserts the `Payment` row's `status`/`failure_reason` alongside the lesson's `expired` status and zero ledger entries).

[2026-09-23 21:50] NOTE — Built the 15-minute unpaid-lesson sweep per advisor item 3/6: `App\Console\Commands\ExpireUnpaidLessons` (`lessons:expire-unpaid`), predicate `status = pending_payment AND created_at <= now() - 15min AND NOT EXISTS(payment WHERE status = captured)` — the `whereDoesntHave('payment', captured)` clause (not just status+age) exists specifically so the sweep cannot race a capture that has already succeeded but whose `confirmed` transition hasn't landed yet; without it the sweep could expire a lesson sitting on captured money with no HOLD, an unrecoverable state. Each matching row is transitioned individually via `LessonStateMachine::transition()` inside a `try/catch (LessonTransitionException)` so a row that moved on between the query and the per-row lock (e.g. `BookLesson` just confirmed it) is silently skipped rather than throwing. Registered in `routes/console.php` as `Schedule::command('lessons:expire-unpaid')->everyMinute()->onOneServer()->withoutOverlapping()`, matching the existing `tutors:check-permits` entry's concurrency guards (R36 e). Added `Lesson::payment(): HasOne` (`app/Models/Lesson.php`) since the predicate and the tests both need it and no relation existed yet. `UNPAID_TIMEOUT_MINUTES = 15` is a class constant per advisor item 6, not a new settings key — PLAN step 3c's own text fixes this number, it isn't a tunable. Five tests in `ExpireUnpaidLessonsTest.php`: expires-when-old-and-unpaid, leaves-alone-when-too-young, never-expires-a-captured-payment, expires-when-only-a-failed-payment-attempt-exists, and idempotent-on-a-second-run (asserts the expired count is identical after running the command twice). `php artisan test --filter=ExpireUnpaidLessonsTest` → 5/5 passed, 12 assertions, 2763ms.

Advice: reject a same-shape JS fix; frame as Option A (server-computed prop reflecting only the tutor's *saved* rate, live-as-you-typed preview removed, logged as a UX loss) vs Option B (a new preview endpoint that calls `TutorProfile::trialPrice()` on a transient unsaved-rate instance, keeping the live-typing UX at the cost of new route/Form-Request/JS-debounce surface); ruled this is a product-shape call, not an architecture one, so CLAUDE.md's own "When unsure" default (simplest option that keeps invariants intact, Owner action logged if genuinely undecided) governs — read R53's literal PLAN wording first, since it may already settle the question, before picking.

[2026-09-23 21:47] DECISION (Claude Code, per advisor item above + R53's literal wording) — Chose Option A. `docs/PLAN.md:11`'s R53 reads in full: "`TutorProfile::trialPrice()` is the ONE place a trial price is computed; the onboarding preview's `100 − pct` rounding is deleted and replaced by a call to it, with a test proving preview and booked price agree across the whole band table including an odd-fils case." R53 mandates deleting the independent formula and replacing it with an actual call to `trialPrice()`; it says nothing about preserving a live-as-you-typed preview of an unsaved rate. Building a preview endpoint (Option B) for that alone would add a new route, Form Request, and JS debounce/loading-state surface for a UX nicety R53 never asked for — against CLAUDE.md's "don't add features beyond what the task requires" and its own default of the simplest option that keeps invariants intact. Implemented: `TutorOnboardingController::show()` now returns `'trialPriceFils' => $profile->trialPrice()?->toFils()` (a genuine call to the one method, reflecting the tutor's last-*saved* rate) in place of the raw `'trialDiscountPct' => Settings::get(...)` value; the dead `Settings` import was removed from the controller since nothing else in it used the facade. `Onboarding.vue` deleted the `trialPriceFils()` computed function entirely (the wrong-formula recompute) and the now-unused `trialDiscountPct` prop, and the rate-step template reads `props.trialPriceFils` directly with copy stating it reflects the saved rate ("Save a new rate to update this") rather than the value currently being typed. **UX loss, logged per the advisor's instruction**: a tutor typing a new hourly rate no longer sees the trial price update live, keystroke by keystroke, before submitting — they see it only after the rate step's own redirect reloads the page with the freshly saved rate. Carried to STATUS §6 for the planner/owner to weigh against restoring it as a CP8/product-polish item (Option B) if the UX loss matters in practice. Checked the three other Vue files the fourth-consult NOTE flagged (`resources/js/pages/tutors/Index.vue`, `tutors/Show.vue`, `match-requests/Index.vue`) via grep: all three type `trial_price` as `string | null` and only display it — none recompute, confirming `App\Services\Search\TutorPresenter.php:61`'s pre-formatted string is already the sole source everywhere else; no changes needed there.

Findings, verbatim from the reviewer's report:

1. FAIL(Medium, app/Actions/Lessons/BookLesson.php:158-169) — A captured payment can be stranded with no ledger HOLD, invisible to both the sweep and `ledger:verify`. How it is reached: `app/Filament/Pages/ManageSettings.php:75` accepts `trial_discount_pct` = 100. `TutorProfile::trialPrice()` (`app/Models/TutorProfile.php:129`) then returns 0. What happens: `BookLesson` opens the lesson (:121), captures 0 successfully (:127) and writes a `captured` Payment row (:148-156). It then calls `transition(Confirmed, hold)` (:159-162). `LedgerService::hold()` throws `LedgerException('A lesson with no price cannot be held.')` (`app/Services/Ledger/LedgerService.php:52-53`). The catch at :164 only handles `LessonTransitionException`, so the `LedgerException` reaches the caller raw instead of as a `BookingException`. Probe result: lesson status `pending_payment`, payment `captured`, 0 ledger rows. One hour later, `lessons:expire-unpaid` still left it in `pending_payment`, because `whereDoesntHave(... Captured)` (`ExpireUnpaidLessons.php:46`) excludes it forever. `ledger:verify` stayed green: `unbalancedLessons()` only looks at lessons that have entries (`LedgerService.php:134-148`). The 09:00 slot was then refused to another learner. The same pair's next booking came back `regular` at 10000, so the trial was used up without ever being delivered. The wider class of bug: any exception between capture and commit leaves the same state (a deadlock, serialization failure or dropped connection inside the transition or `hold()` is enough). The mirror case is `Payment::create` failing after a successful capture (:148) — then there is no Payment row, the sweep expires the lesson after 15 minutes, and the captured money has no record anywhere. The existing test documents the state rather than preventing it: `BookLessonTest.php:325-360` asserts the "sweep won" end state and nothing flags or reconciles it. Why it blocks: once CP5 brings non-zero real captures, this is captured money outside the ledger, a breach of invariant #1 and R52/R53; under R50 that is a halt condition. Suggested fix: catch `Throwable` after capture and move the lesson to a detectable state or record a reconciliation marker; reject a zero price before capture, or cap `trial_discount_pct` below 100; extend `ledger:verify` (or add a check) to flag any `captured` payment whose lesson has no HOLD.
2. FAIL(Low, app/Console/Commands/ExpireUnpaidLessons.php:49) — Sweep has no captured-payment re-check after it takes the row lock. `LessonStateMachine::transition($lesson, Expired)` is called with no check inside the lock, so a payment captured after the query at :44-46 still gets its lesson expired. Probe: inserted the captured Payment during the sweep's own locked re-read; the lesson ended `expired` with its payment `captured`. A re-check alone would not close the gap — `BookLesson` writes the captured Payment (:148) without locking the lesson row, so the Payment insert needs to move into the confirm transition's locked closure too. Why Low now: the fake gateway captures instantly, so a capture landing 15+ minutes after lesson creation is unrealistic in CP3; becomes a real risk with CP5 gateway latency or 3DS — flag for CP5.

FAIL(Low, app/Console/Commands/ExpireUnpaidLessons.php:47) — `->each()` skips rows past 1000 in a single run. `each()` pages by offset, and each expiry removes the row from the `pending_payment` filter, so the next page's offset jumps past rows that were never processed. Probe: with 2500 eligible lessons, one run expired 1500 and left 1000 pending. It heals itself because the sweep runs every minute, but a run's output undercounts. Use `lazyById()` or `chunkById()`.
